A sliding-window log-likelihood fuses recent tactile frames to reduce single-frame ambiguity, while a potential-field-guided proposal steers particles away from hand--object penetration. Symmetry-aware resampling preserves multiple plausible modes. Experiments on an Allegro Hand V5 with five objects show lower normalized ADD-S than tactile-only geometric, particle-filter, and learning baselines, and ablations confirm the benefits of temporal fusion, potential guidance, and mode preservation.

</details>


### [227] [Iterative Grasp Pose Refinement: A Deep Reinforcement Learning Approach for 2D Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17628)
*Amir Arsalan Nematollahi,Shayan Ahmadi,Mehdi Tale Masouleh,Ahmad Kalhor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于强化学习的机器人抓取优化框架，将关键点物体表示与深度Q网络结合，通过迭代优化将失败的抓取转化为成功抓取，在300个Dex-Net数据集物体上实现100%成功率，并通过仿真到真实环境迁移验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要具备理解和操作物体的能力，这需要紧凑、可解释且可泛化的表示方法。传统几何方法在处理复杂接触操作任务时存在局限性，特别是在处理难以抓取的物体方面。

Method: 采用基于强化学习的框架，将关键点物体表示与深度Q网络(DQN)集成。使用模拟环境中的2D俯视图，几何算法生成初始抓取候选，然后通过该框架进行迭代优化，将失败抓取转化为成功抓取。

Result: 在Dex-Net数据集300个物体上使用UR5操作器进行实验，框架对几何方法先前认为无法抓取的物体实现了100%成功率。通过Delta并联机器人的物理实验进一步验证了仿真到真实环境的迁移能力，成功操作了先前无法抓取的物体。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明强化学习在解决机器人抓取挑战方面的有效性，为接触丰富的操作任务提供了可扩展、适应性强的解决方案，证明了将强化学习与几何方法结合能够显著提升机器人抓取性能。

Abstract: Developing robots capable of understanding and manipulating objects requires compact, interpretable, and generalizable representations. This work proposes a reinforcement learning-based framework for robotic grasp refinement, integrating keypoint-based object representations with a Deep Q-Network (DQN). Using 2D overhead images captured in a simulated environment, a geometric-based algorithm generates initial grasp candidates, which are iteratively refined by the proposed framework, transforming failed grasps into successful ones. Experiments conducted on 300 objects from the Dex-Net dataset using a UR5 manipulator demonstrate the framework's effectiveness, achieving a 100% success rate on objects previously deemed ungraspable by geometrical methods. The framework's sim-to-real transferability is further validated through physical experiments on a Delta parallel robot, where a refined grasp successfully manipulates an object that was previously ungraspable. The findings underscore the effectiveness of reinforcement learning in addressing challenges in robotic grasping, offering a scalable and adaptable solution for contact-rich manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [228] [OVIP-SG: Open-Vocabulary Instance-Preserving Scene Graphs for Mapping and Retrieval of Small, Fine-Grained Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17633)
*Tianjing Hao,Haiyu Lan,Angsong Li,Cheng Chen,Enyu Li,Jiarui Yang,Yuning Su,Peiwen Lin,Wang Chuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OVIP-SG是一个统一的开放世界3D场景图框架，通过实例保持语义建图、功能场景划分及语言引导的细小物体检索，在保持实例一致性的同时提升检测与检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将开放词汇感知整合到物体级3D场景图中是一把双刃剑：视觉语言检测器虽能恢复闭集模型忽略的长尾类别和细小精细物体，但往往将大表面碎片化、将小物体合并到邻近大物体中，损害实例级一致性与建图保真度；此外现有方法难以检索先前未映射目标或判定查询对象是否缺失，阻碍了鲁棒的具身开放世界导航与探索。

Method: 提出OVIP-SG统一框架，利用视觉语言模型(VLM)枚举场景特定类别以实现鲁棒开放世界检测；采用对称3D交并比(IoU)关联与面积加权特征融合保留小独立实例；基于VLM推断物体功能对场景进行紧凑功能区域划分；设计四阶段级联检索流水线，结合体素投票并根据探索覆盖度判断目标缺失。

Result: 在Replica数据集的统一评估协议下，OVIP-SG在类平均准确率(mAcc)上比ConceptGraphs高6.31分，在频率加权mIoU(F-mIoU)上高5.15分，实现类无关原生实例全景质量(PQ)为0.398；将搜索区域缩小至室内地板面积的21.8%，目标存在分类的平衡准确率达到0.773。真实机器人实验进一步验证了其实际有效性。

Conclusion: OVIP-SG通过统一框架解决了开放词汇感知中实例碎片化、小目标合并及查询目标缺失判定等难题，在Replica基准上显著优于现有方法，并在真实机器人实验中验证了其实用性。

Abstract: Integrating open-vocabulary perception into object-level 3D scene graphs is a double-edged sword. While vision-language detectors recover long-tail categories and small, fine-grained objects overlooked by closed-set models, they also tend to fragment large surfaces and merge small objects into larger neighboring objects, compromising instance-level consistency and undermining mapping fidelity. Moreover, existing methods struggle to retrieve previously unmapped targets or determine whether a queried object is absent, hindering robust embodied open-world navigation and exploration. We present OVIP-SG, a unified framework for instance-preserving semantic mapping, functional scene partitioning, and language-guided small, fine-grained object retrieval. OVIP-SG uses a vision-language model (VLM) to enumerate scene-specific categories for robust open-world detection. Symmetric 3D Intersection over Union (IoU) association and area-weighted feature fusion preserve small independent instances, while VLM-inferred object functions partition scenes into compact functional search regions. A four-stage cascaded retrieval pipeline further incorporates voxel voting and determines target absence from exploration coverage. Under a unified evaluation protocol on Replica, OVIP-SG outperforms ConceptGraphs by 6.31 points in class-mean accuracy (mAcc) and 5.15 points in frequency-weighted mIoU (F-mIoU) while achieving a class-agnostic native-instance Panoptic Quality (PQ) of 0.398. It reduces the search area to 21.8% of the indoor floor space and reaches 0.773 balanced accuracy for object-presence classification. Real-world robotic experiments further demonstrate its practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [229] [Collective Ranking of Environmental Signals through Gaussian Belief Propagation in a Patrolling Robot Swarm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17690)
*Zachary R. Madin,Connor York,Jonathan Lawry,Edmund R. Hunt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Multi-robot patrolling requires a team to visit all areas of an environment at regular intervals, typically minimising idleness. A practical extension, motivated by security and environmental monitoring, is to additionally form a collective ranking of all patrol locations by some measured signal, a generalisation of the best-of-n problem to the many-option, continuous-valued regime. We observe that the patrol graph admits a natural dual interpretation: it is simultaneously the topology that dictates agent movement and a factor graph over which spatial beliefs can be propagated. Exploiting this equivalence, we apply Gaussian Belief Propagation (GBP), a graph-based algorithm, to collective ranking using unary measurement factors at visited nodes and pairwise smoothness factors along patrol edges. We compare GBP against simple and visit-count-weighted averaging across a range of sensor-noise conditions in simulation, and validate the approach on four Leo Rovers tracking a propagating radio signal in an office lobby. GBP outperforms both baselines on ranking accuracy, mean squared error, and time to consensus. We find that as noise increases and the task becomes harder, GBP degrades gracefully in simulation while both averaging methods degrade substantially. Hardware trials reproduce the same performance ordering on a real propagating radio signal, supporting the practical relevance of the simulated results.

</details>


### [230] [Force-Based Offset Estimation for Keyed Peg-in-Hole Assembly Using Local Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17691)
*Chandra Yuvesh Aubeeluck,Abilash Philip Madavath,Augustin Raju,Nicolas Pyschny,Felix Hackelöer,Florian Zwanzig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于力的偏移估计方法，结合KNN-高斯过程混合回归器，通过区分接触机制和视觉验证，将键孔装配成功率从67%提升至87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 键槽装配任务对几何约束要求严格，在不确定环境下对抓取位姿偏差高度敏感，需要精确的偏移估计方法来提高装配成功率。

Method: 提出力偏移估计方法并嵌入感知-验证-插入流程：(1)使用局部KNN-高斯过程混合回归器直接根据腕部力/力矩测量值估计残余偏移；(2)区分硬碰撞和导向倒角插入两种接触机制，通过接触窗口持续时间阈值进行分类；(3)结合KNN与确定性搜索，利用抓取后单目视觉验证结果提高回归器模型精度。

Result: 在倒角销插入中实现精确径向偏移估计；协作机器人手臂实验表明，应用该流程后插入成功率从67%提高到87%。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于力的偏移估计方法通过多机制区分和视觉辅助，显著提高了键槽装配任务的精度和成功率，为不确定环境下的精密装配提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Key-keyway assembly tasks impose strict geometric constraints and are highly sensitive to grasp pose deviations in uncertain environments. This work presents a force-based offset estimation method for keyed peg-in-hole assembly, embedded within a perception-validation-insertion pipeline. Residual misalignment is estimated directly from wrist force/torque measurements using a local KNN-Gaussian Process hybrid regressor. The framework distinguishes between two contact regimes, hard collision and guided chamfer insertion, and routes inference to a dedicated model for each. Regime classification is achieved via a contact-window duration threshold. KNN combined with a deterministic search using the results of a post-grasp monocular visual validation contributes to an increased accuracy of the regressor model. This approach achieves accurate radial offset estimation in chamfered peg insertion, during a keypoint detection-based pick and place application. Experiments using the integrated force/torque sensor of a collaborative robot arm showed an increase in insertion success rate from 67% to 87% after the pipeline was applied.

</details>


### [231] [Dijkstra as an Oracle for Online Stochastic Shortest Path Navigation with Provable Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17703)
*Mansur M. Arief,Ali Akarma,Ahmad Alfan Alfian Irfan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DORA算法，在比因果条件更弱的条件下使Dijkstra算法成为精确规划引擎，实现高效安全的机器人在线导航与路径规划，计算量减少4.5-19.3倍，碰撞次数降低17倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与人协作的移动机器人需在未知真实穿越成本和不完美的执行条件下以低成本到达目标。精确求解随机最短路径问题的规划器（如值迭代）计算量随地图直径增长，而Dijkstra算法快速但通常在随机转移下被认为不精确。需要高效且安全的规划方法。

Method: 研究表明在确定化地图上归约成本非负的弱条件下，Dijkstra算法仍可保持精确性。基于此提出DORA在线学习算法：每集调用固定次数最短路径预言机，不估计转移核，当动态障碍物接触概率需满足预算时添加对数生存权重。

Result: 三项基准实验（网格世界导航、定向钻井、无人机监视）显示：DORA匹配已知真实转移核的乐观值迭代，规划工作量减少4.5-19.3倍；相对确定化重规划方法碰撞次数降低17倍；接触率保持在跨越两个数量级的预算范围内。

Conclusion: 最短路径搜索支持安全高效的在线导航和路径规划任务，DORA算法在计算效率和安全性方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Mobile robots that operate in side by side with humans and critical facilities must reach their goals at low cost, despite often unknown true traversal costs of the map apriori and imperfect actuation. Planners that solve the underlying stochastic shortest path problem exactly, such as value iteration, require computation that grows with the diameter of the map, whereas Dijkstra's algorithm is fast but is usually considered inexact once transitions are stochastic. This study shows that Dijkstra's algorithm can remain an exact planning engine under a condition that is much weaker than the causality condition often invoked in the literature, namely nonnegativity of a reduced cost defined on the determinized map. Building on this characterization, an online learner DORA (Dijkstra Oracle Reduced-cost Algorithm) is proposed for robot navigation that calls a shortest path oracle a fixed number of times per episode, never estimates a transition kernel, and adds a logarithmic survival weight when the probability of contact with a dynamic obstacle must stay within a budget. In the numerical experiments involving three other benchmarks that cover grid world navigation, directional drilling, and drone surveillance, the learner matches optimistic value iteration that is given the true transition kernel while performing 4.5 to 19.3 times less planner work, reduces contacts during learning by a factor of seventeen relative to determinize and replan, and keeps the contact rate within budgets that span two orders of magnitude. These results indicate that shortest path search supports safe and efficient online navigation and path planning tasks.

</details>


### [232] [CompCPZ: Preserving Multi-Modal Intent in Language-Guided Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17717)
*Zhen Zhang,Ahmad Hafez,Peng Xie,Yanliang Huang,Wenyuan Wu,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CompCPZ，一种代数层方法，解决语言条件机器人策略中析取指令（如"靠近红盘或蓝盘"）被错误简化为单一连通集合的问题，通过递归组合约束多项式区域来保持多模态可行性表示，显著提升任务成功率并实现真实机器人迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言条件机器人策略在处理析取指令时存在结构性缺陷：将析取指令（如"靠近红盘或蓝盘"）简化为单一连通集合，导致机器人可能到达两个目标之间的质心，几何上看似成功却语义上未满足任一析取项，这种"静默语义失败"暴露了多模态表示能力不足和运行时模式不确定性下的规划退化问题。

Method: 提出CompCPZ（可组合约束多项式区域），一种代数层封装方法：沿语言解析树递归组合每个原语的约束多项式区域封闭，采用分布自由一致覆盖技术，实现亚毫秒级运行时，保持多模态析取表示。

Result: 在闭环ManiSkill3桌面操作基准测试中，CompCPZ优于凸集基线、多峰解码器和零样本视觉语言动作模型（1900/1918配对胜利，p<<10^-30）；相同编译器无需重新调整即可迁移到Unitree Go2四足机器人运动捕捉下的真实机器人试验。

Conclusion: 组合语言接地应评估表示的可行性集是否保持用户意图的连通分量结构，而不仅仅是达到解码目标，CompCPZ通过代数层实现多模态析取表示的恢复，为语言条件机器人策略提供了新的评估标准和方法论。

Abstract: A robot asked to "place the cup near the red plate or the blue plate" may reach the centroid between them and appear geometrically successful, while satisfying neither disjunct of the instruction. This silent semantic failure exposes a structural limitation of language-conditioned robot policies: representations that collapse a disjunctive instruction into a single connected set cannot preserve all feasible modes, and planners that commit to one action degrade under run-time mode uncertainty. We address this limitation with CompCPZ, a sound algebraic layer that language-conditioned learning systems wrap to recover multi-modal disjunctive representation, recursively composing per-primitive constrained polynomial zonotope enclosures along the language parse tree with distribution-free conformal coverage and sub-millisecond runtime. On a closed-loop ManiSkill3 tabletop-manipulation benchmark, CompCPZ outperforms convex set baselines, multi-peak decoders, and a zero-shot vision-language-action model (1,900/1,918 paired wins, p << 10^(-30)); the same compiler also transfers without retuning to planar real-robot trials on a Unitree Go2 quadruped under motion capture. These results suggest that compositional language grounding should be evaluated not only by reaching a decoded target, but by whether the represented feasibility set preserves the connected-component structure of the user's intent.

</details>


### [233] [Stability Control for Real World Testing in Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17779)
*Phillip Pitschi,Simon Sagmeister,Frederik Werner,Markus Lienkamp,Boris Lohmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于自动驾驶的综合性稳定性控制系统，通过ESC、SC和CS协同工作保障极限工况下的安全，提供开源实现，并通过仿真与实车实验验证了其在危急情况下维持车辆稳定及扩展操作边界的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆在极限操控下易受路况或天气影响变得不稳定，而大多数控制算法假设车辆行为稳定，在失稳时可能失效。特别是在无安全驾驶员的昂贵车辆（如自动驾驶赛车）运行中存在巨大安全风险，需要保障车辆在动态极限下的安全操作。

Method: 设计了一套综合稳定性控制系统，包含电子稳定性控制(ESC)、滑移控制(SC)和反向转向系统(CS)。系统通过调整来自上层运动控制器的转向和制动指令来维持车辆稳定性，并通过仿真及全尺寸实车实验进行验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，该稳定性控制系统在危急情况下能够成功保持车辆稳定性，并扩展了车辆的操作可行区域。

Conclusion: 该系统有效解决了自动驾驶在极限工况下的稳定性问题，为确保无安全驾驶员情况下的安全提供了方案，且开源代码方便集成。

Abstract: Controlling an autonomous vehicle at the limits of handling is a challenging task. Due to external influences, such as road conditions or weather, a vehicle can easily become unstable. Since most control algorithms assume stable vehicle behavior, they might fail in these situations. Especially when operating expensive vehicles without a safety driver on board, as in autonomous racing, this poses a significant challenge. To enable safe operation at the vehicle's dynamic limits, we present a comprehensive stability control system that safeguards motion control algorithms in autonomous driving. The proposed system consists of an electronic stability control (ESC), a slip control (SC), and a countersteer system (CS), which collectively adapt steering and brake commands from the motion controller to maintain vehicle stability. We validate our approach through both simulation and experiments on a real-world, full-scale vehicle. The results show that the stability control system maintains vehicle stability in critical situations and extends the operational feasible region. To simplify integration, we provide an open-source implementation at github.com/TUMFTM/tam-stability-control.

</details>


### [234] [Effector-Centric NMPC of Tiltable-Multirotors for Offset-Free Omnidirectional Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17819)
*Jinjie Li,Yicheng Chen,Johannes Kübel,Haokun Liu,Junichiro Sugihara,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可倾斜多旋翼飞行器的六自由度空中操作框架。研究分析了四旋翼可倾斜配置在螺旋桨尺寸和悬停效率间的平衡，提出基于执行器的非线性模型预测控制(NMPC)框架，实现了含奇点穿越的全方向操作。实机实验验证了90度空翻、推白板、连续360度阀门旋转等操作能力，系统以100Hz在自研飞行器上全机载运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中操作可将机器人作业扩展到以往无法触及的空中环境。相比配备机械臂的空中系统，可倾斜多旋翼通过倾斜推力方向直接产生六自由度 wrench，实现高效移动和全方向操作能力。

Method: 1. 设计分析：证明四旋翼可倾斜配置在螺旋桨无干涉尺寸和不同姿态下的悬停效率间达到最佳平衡，其零空间冗余对物理约束下的奇点穿越至关重要
2. 执行器放置：向上末端执行器放置在地面间隙和可用 wrench 间取得有利权衡
3. 扰动补偿：提出包含修正积分项和基于加速度的外力估计器的双策略
4. 控制框架：开发集成设计选择、奇点处理和扰动补偿的统一执行器中心化NMPC框架

Result: 框架在自研可倾斜四旋翼上以100Hz全机载运行。真实世界实验包括90度步态空翻旋转、白板推动和连续360度阀门旋转，证明基于wrench的全方向操作的可行性及单自由度每臂可倾斜四旋翼上的奇点穿越能力。

Conclusion: 可倾斜多旋翼通过直接产生六自由度wrench实现了高效移动和全方向操作。四旋翼配置在干扰和效率间达到最佳平衡，配合向上执行器放置和双扰动补偿策略，所开发的NMPC框架成功实现了含奇点穿越的空中操作，验证了该技术方案的实际可行性。

Abstract: Aerial manipulation extends robotic operations to previously inaccessible aerial environments. Unlike arm-equipped aerial systems, tiltable-multirotors can directly generate six-degree-of-freedom wrenches through their flight bases, enabling both efficient movement and omnidirectional operation by tilting the thrust direction.
  This work presents a design analysis and a wrench-based control framework for tiltable-multirotors in aerial manipulation. We show that a four-rotor tiltable configuration provides a balance between interference-free propeller sizing and hovering efficiency across different attitudes, and its null-space redundancy is crucial for traversing singular configurations under physical constraints. We further show that an upward end-effector placement yields a favorable trade-off between geometric clearance and available wrench. To address disturbances, we propose a dual strategy consisting of a modified integral term for model error and an acceleration-based estimator for external wrenches. Building on these insights, we develop an effector-centric nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) framework that integrates design choices, singularity handling, and disturbance compensation into a unified formulation.
  The proposed framework runs fully onboard at 100 Hz on a custom-built tiltable-quadrotor. Real-world experiments, including a 90-deg step cartwheel rotation, whiteboard pushing, and continuous 360-deg valve turning, demonstrate the feasibility of wrench-based omnidirectional manipulation with singularity traversal on a one-DoF-per-arm tiltable-quadrotor.

</details>


### [235] [Jetson-ORB-SLAM3: Accuracy-Preserving GPU Implementation for Edge Computing Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17874)
*Rajat Roy,Aditya Arun Kumar Yadav,Hardik Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ORB-SLAM3在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上的高精度GPU实现方案，通过算法级复现CPU端ORB检测器（关键特征点94.7%一致、描述子99.9%一致）保持轨迹精度，并基于TensorRT将CNN回环检测加速至2.2ms/次。系统在7W功耗下实现32FPS单目-惯性SLAM，欧几里得数据集轨迹误差<0.10cm，证明边缘设备可实现无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低功耗边缘平台中视觉惯性SLAM受限于密集特征提取与回环检测的计算成本。现有GPU移植方案通过简化ORB检测器牺牲精度，导致特征集变化进而影响轨迹估计；同时CNN回环检测因框架限制难以在嵌入式平台运行。

Method: 构建视觉前端GPU流水线（含算法一致性ORB实现）与CPU后端分离架构：1) GPU端精确复现CPUORB检测流程；2) 基于libnvinfer FP16引擎优化CosPlace ResNet-50回环模型；3) 在Jetson Orin Nano与桌面端交叉验证四种配置（GPU/CPU×硬件）的轨迹一致性。

Result: 四维配置在EuroC数据集上平均绝对轨迹误差差异<0.10cm；TUM-VI序列5/6达亚厘米精度，KITTI序列9/11相对平移误差<1%；回环检测较通用ONNX执行器提速180倍；单目-惯性模式持续32FPS运行。

Conclusion: 证明通过算法级硬件适配可实现无损加速，突破边缘SLAM的精度-性能矛盾；为低功耗平台部署学习型感知系统提供可复现范式，显著提升嵌入式SLAM实用性。

Abstract: Visual-inertial SLAM on low-power edge platforms is constrained by the cost of dense feature extraction and loop closure. Prior GPU ports of ORB-SLAM trade accuracy for speed by approximating the ORB detector, altering the feature set and therefore the estimated trajectory. We present an accuracy-preserving GPU implementation of ORB-SLAM3 for the NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano, whose GPU ORB front end reproduces the reference CPU detector algorithmically to 94.7% exact keypoint agreement and 99.9% descriptor bit agreement. This work also makes CNN-based loop closure edge-viable through native TensorRT. The visual front end (feature extraction) is offloaded to the GPU while the mapping and optimization back end is kept on the CPU, matching each computation to the hardware it suits. The accuracy is verified by comparing four configurations: the GPU pipeline and the unmodified CPU reference, each run on both the Jetson Orin Nano and a desktop. On EuRoC dataset, all four agree to within 0.10cm in mean absolute trajectory error (SE(3)), so neither the GPU port nor the change of hardware shifts the estimated trajectory. The GPU-versus-CPU comparison is reproducible on TUM-VI and KITTI datasets, so the acceleration is accuracy-preserving rather than approximate. The proposed implementation is competitive with published ORB-SLAM3 on EuRoC, attains sub-centimeter accuracy on five of the six TUM-VI room sequences, and reaches sub-1% relative translation error on nine of eleven KITTI sequences. For loop closure, the generic ONNX-Runtime CUDA/TensorRT execution providers are unusable with our CosPlace ResNet-50 on the embedded platform, whereas a native libnvinfer FP16 engine reduces per-query inference to 2.2ms, a 180x speedup. Learned place recognition therefore runs concurrently with tracking on a 7W device. In monocular-inertial mode the system sustains 32FPS mean over the eleven EuRoC sequences.

</details>


### [236] [ControlledShifts: Towards Standardizing Robustness Evaluation in Trajectory Prediction Under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17882)
*Ingrid navarro,Pablo Ortega-Kral,Yutong Duan,Jonathan Francis,Jean Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ControlledShifts框架与基准测试套件，通过系统化地将轨迹预测数据集划分为分布内/分布外分区，并提供统一的鲁棒性评分指标，用于评估模型在不同分布偏移场景下的预测质量和稳定性。实验对主流Transformer架构进行基准测试，揭示了不同容量模型在处理潜在相关性和环境结构方面的关键差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹预测对自动驾驶安全至关重要，但基于学习的预测器在遇到训练数据中未充分表示的场景时性能显著下降。现有方法通过以数据为中心或测试时适应的方式缓解分布偏移退化，但通常在碎片化的泛化轴上验证，缺乏跨偏移场景标准化比较模型鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 提出ControlledShifts框架，采用共享的特征化-划分公式系统化地将现有轨迹数据集重新划分为分布内（已见）和分布外（未见）分区。特征化函数固定基准测试探查的变化轴，划分函数固定如何保留该轴的尾部。套件包含三个针对关键拓扑和行为分布偏移的基准测试。此外，提出统一鲁棒性评分聚合多维度性能指标，从预测质量（相对性能增益）和预测稳定性（偏移下的性能保持）两个互补维度评估模型。

Result: 利用ControlledShifts对突出的基于Transformer的架构进行基准测试，揭示了不同容量模型在处理潜在相关性和环境结构方面的关键差异。实验展示了该框架在系统化评估模型鲁棒性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: ControlledShifts框架填补了轨迹预测领域标准化鲁棒性评估的空白，通过系统化的分布偏移基准测试和统一评分机制，为比较模型在多样化偏移场景下的鲁棒性提供了有效工具，有助于推动更可靠的自动驾驶轨迹预测技术发展。

Abstract: Trajectory prediction is central to safety in autonomous driving, yet learning-based predictors tend to degrade sharply when encountering scenarios poorly represented by their training data. Many methods attempt to mitigate distribution shift degradation through data-centric or test-time adaptation approaches; however, they are typically validated along fragmented axes of generalization, leaving the field without a standardized way to compare robustness across shifts a model may encounter.
  To address this, we introduce ControlledShifts, a framework and benchmark suite that systematically re-splits existing trajectory datasets into in-distribution (seen) and out-of-distribution (unseen) partitions, via a shared characterization-and-splitting formulation, in which a characterization function fixes the axis of variation a benchmark probes and a splitting function fixes how the tail of that axis is withheld. The suite comprises three benchmarks targeting key topological and behavioral distribution shifts. Furthermore, to aggregate multi-dimensional performance metrics across these benchmarks, we propose a unified robustness score that evaluates models along two complementary dimensions: prediction quality (relative performance gain) and prediction stability (performance preservation under shift). We showcase ControlledShifts by benchmarking prominent transformer-based architectures, exposing critical differences in how models of varying capacities handle latent relevance and environmental structure.

</details>


### [237] [PRISM: Precision and contact-rich Real-world Industrial Skill dataset with Multimodal sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17962)
*Tengbo Yu,Jiahao Wu,Hanning Wang,Rui Chen,Chuanhou Liu,Chuang Sun,Hangxin Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM数据集，一个面向工业接触密集型操作的大规模多模态机器人学习数据集，填补了现有数据集在工业装配场景中的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习数据集主要关注短时距、低接触任务（如抓取放置），缺乏工业装配所需的精度控制、力/力矩调节、触觉反馈和多模态感知能力，难以支持复杂的工业操作场景。

Method: 构建PRISM数据集，涵盖25种以上操作任务（如电子元件插拔、传送带分拣），包含5000多条轨迹（45小时遥操作演示），采用同步多视角RGB-D、力/力矩、触觉和机器人状态测量设备采集数据。

Result: PRISM提供了高精度工业约束下的多模态感知与控制真实基准，包含丰富的机械约束信息，能够支持接触密集型操作的泛化研究。

Conclusion: PRISM数据集为工业制造环境中的接触密集型、泛化性强的机器人操作研究奠定了基础，促进了多模态感知与控制技术的发展，数据集已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: Recent progress in robotic learning has been fueled by large-scale datasets collected in everyday environments. However, most existing datasets emphasize short-horizon, low-contact tasks such as pick-and-place, and therefore do not capture the precision control, force/torque or tactile regulation, and multimodal feedback required for industrial assembly. To address this gap, we introduce PRISM, a large-scale multimodal dataset for contact-rich industrial operations. The dataset spans more than 25 manipulation tasks (e.g., electronic components plug/unplug, conveyor-based sorting) and covers diverse mechanical constraints. PRISM includes more than 5,000 trajectories totaling 45 hours of teleoperated demonstrations, recorded using synchronized multi-view RGB-D, force/torque, tactile, and robot-state measurements. In contrast to datasets collected in household or laboratory settings, PRISM provides a realistic benchmark for multimodal perception and control under high-precision industrial constraints, and serves as a foundation for contact-rich, generalizable manipulation in real-world manufacturing environments. The dataset is open-sourced at: https://tengbo-yu.github.io/PRISM/

</details>


### [238] [Hydra-0: Action Flow for Generalist World Modeling and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18077)
*Hongyu Li,Bowen Wen,Xinghao Zhu,Yixuan Wang,Yilun Du,Yunzhu Li,George Konidaris,Stan Birchfield,Soha Pouya,Chenran Li,Yan Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Hydra-0，一个基于动作流的通用世界模型，将机器人动作表示为像素运动。该方法在机器人运动误差和物体运动误差上分别降低了90.4%和60.2%，支持零样本组合和数据高效适应，并在RoboLab基准上达到r=0.96的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习面临数据异构性、跨任务泛化和样本效率等挑战。传统方法缺乏统一的表示框架来整合不同来源的训练数据、进行策略评估并实现机器人控制。本文旨在探索一种共享的视觉控制接口，以解决这些核心问题。

Method: 提出Hydra-0世界模型，以动作流（像素运动）作为条件化输入。该模型通过共享视觉接口学习跨不同躯体、任务、环境和视频生成后端的动作后果。训练后的动作头将潜在特征映射为可执行动作，无需任务特定的专家机器人演示。此外，发现了一种逆向模式：从人类演示中转移的期望物体流预测兼容的机器人运动。

Result: 1) 相比动作条件基线，机器人运动误差降低90.4%，物体运动误差降低60.2%；2) 支持零样本组合和数据高效适应；3) 在RoboLab基准上，重放成功率与参考成功率之间的皮尔逊相关系数达r=0.96；4) 发现动作流接口的逆向模式，可从人类演示预测机器人运动。

Conclusion: 动作流作为共享控制接口，成功连接了异构训练数据、开环策略评估和机器人控制。Hydra-0模型展示了跨任务和环境的泛化能力，无需任务特定演示即可实现控制，为通用机器人学习提供了新的范式。

Abstract: We introduce Hydra-0, a generalist world model conditioned on action flow, which represents robot actions as pixel motion. This shared visual interface enables generalist world modeling and control by learning action consequences across embodiments, tasks, environments, and video-generation backbones. Our best configuration achieves 90.4% lower robot-motion error and 60.2% lower object-motion error than our action-conditioned baseline, while supporting zero-shot composition and data-efficient adaptation. On the RoboLab benchmark, Hydra-0 achieves a Pearson correlation of r=0.96 between replayed and reference success rates. Finally, we uncover an emergent inverse mode of this interface: a world action model that predicts compatible robot motion from desired object flow transferred from a human demonstration. A trained action head maps the resulting latent features to executable actions without requiring task-specific expert robot demonstrations. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of action flow as a shared control interface connecting heterogeneous training data, open-loop policy evaluation, and robot control.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [An O-RAN-Assisted MARL Approach for Dynamic Sidelink and Infrastructure Selection in V2X Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17210)
*Maria Katarine Santana Barbosa,Kelvin Lopes Dias*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对6G车联网中侧链路（SL）通信带来的干扰和资源竞争问题，提出基于Open RAN的多智能体强化学习（MARL）资源管理系统。通过集中训练、分散执行和聚类策略，优化侧链路与网络传输选择，显著降低干扰、资源损失和时延。实验结果表明，相比单智能体方案，纯车辆场景下平均损失降低21%、时延降低19%；车辆与易受伤害道路用户共存场景下，损失和时延分别降低18%和30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来6G车联网将依赖V2X场景中的侧链路（SL）传输，但SL直接通信会加剧车辆间及车辆与其他ITS实体间的干扰，导致V2V通信和VRU上行资源恶化甚至资源饥饿。现有方案多聚焦于资源分配和配对选择，缺乏对通信模式和整个网络的综合考虑，因此需要一种更全面的解决方案。

Method: 本文利用Open RAN管理V2X通信，并构建多智能体强化学习（MARL）资源感知系统。Open RAN提供全局网络视图的控制回路和面向机器学习模型的开放接口框架，支持资源决策。MARL模型旨在通过最优选择侧链路或网络传输来减轻干扰、优化资源利用并提升服务质量。为降低系统复杂度，采用聚类策略，每个智能体管理一组配对而非每个配对单独分配智能体。方案采用集中训练与分散执行（CTDE）方法，结合离线训练和离策略（off-policy）学习，每个智能体存储经验以供微调。

Result: 实验结果表明，在仅车辆场景下，MARL方案使平均损失降低21%，时延降低19%。在车辆与VRU共存场景下，与单智能体方案相比，损失和时延分别降低18%和30%，验证了多智能体协作和聚类策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于Open RAN的多智能体强化学习资源管理系统能够有效缓解V2X通信中的干扰问题，优化资源分配并提升服务质量，尤其在多场景共存时表现出显著的性能优势，为6G车联网的资源管理提供了可行且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Future applications in the 6G-based Internet of Vehicles will leverage sidelink (SL) transmissions in Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) scenarios. However, SL-based direct communication can significantly increase interference among vehicles and between vehicles and other entities of the Intelligent Transportation System. Thus, both Vehicle-to-Vehicle communications and Vulnerable Road Users (VRUs) uplink resources may be degraded or subject to starvation. Existing solutions primarily focus on improving resource allocation and pair selection. Nonetheless, they lack a comprehensive approach to tackle the communication modes and the entire network. To address these challenges, this paper leverages Open RAN to manage V2X communication and proposes a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) resource-aware system. Open RAN provides control loops through a global view of the network and also an open interface-based framework for machine learning models applied to resource decision-making. Meanwhile, the MARL model aims to mitigate interference, optimize resource usage, and enhance quality of service by optimally selecting between sidelink and network transmissions. To reduce system complexity, this work employs a clustering strategy. Each agent manages a group of pairs, rather than assigning one agent to each pair. The solution supports this design by adopting a centralized training with decentralized execution approach, empowered by Open RAN. The strategy uses offline training and an off-policy approach, in which each agent stores experience for fine-tuning. Results indicate that the MARL approach reduces average loss by 21% and latency by 19% in Vehicle-only scenarios. In coexistence VRU scenarios, loss and latency drop by 18% and 30%, respectively, compared to the single-agent approach.

</details>


### [240] [LoRIS: LoRaWAN-based IoT Platform for Sustainability Monitoring in Hotels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17467)
*Yash Pandey,Angus Gray,Reza Serati,Oscar Zhu,Emil Juvan,Anna Zinn,Danyelle Greene,Qingqing Chen,Sarah MacInnes,Siamak Layeghy,Sara Dolnicar,Marius Portmann*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The hospitality sector is a major source of global greenhouse gas emissions, water stress, and waste generation, yet sustainability reporting in hotels remains constrained by coarse, manually collected operational data. We present LoRIS (LoRaWAN-based IoT platform for sustainability monitoring in hotels), a LoRaWAN-based sensing system that delivers high-resolution measurements of resource consumption, environmental conditions, and guest behaviour across geographically distributed hotel properties. The architecture follows the canonical LoRaWAN reference model and is built for the operational realities of hospitality deployments: restrictive hotel IT policies, guest privacy expectations, rapid and reversible installation, and multi-year battery operation. Privacy-by-design guides modality selection and deployment zoning, and end-to-end encryption protects data from sensor to dashboard. This system has been running since February 2022 and currently spans 850 sensors of 19 types across 21 sites in Australia and Slovenia, covering both the AU915 and EU868 regulatory regions. The platform has generated over 202 million sensor records and ingests approximately 245,000 uplink messages per day on managed serverless infrastructure. Our system has been successfully used for seven field studies spanning food waste, energy consumption, and water consumption, including controlled intervention experiments that measure environmental outcomes and guest satisfaction in parallel. This system shows that LoRaWAN sensing can be deployed at scale in operational hotels without compromising guest experience or privacy.

</details>


### [241] [Predict Before Replay: Joint FEC and Flight Control for Reliable Scale-Up Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17503)
*Fan Yang,Jiaqi Liu,Tao Jiang,Zhan Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出PREFACE，一种针对时间相关突发错误的预FEC控制器，通过贝叶斯滤波器和FEC强度调整，显著改善加速器互连网络的可靠性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模加速器互连以数百Gbps速率传输延迟敏感数据，现有可靠性流水线依赖FEC和重传机制。在高线速下，延迟反馈导致后续数据帧进入重传窗口前，残留错误才被发现，标准重传会将单个损坏数据帧放大为后缀重传，严重影响系统性能。

Method: 设计PREFACE预FEC控制器，采用双状态贝叶斯滤波器将校正符号观测转换为下一数据帧突发后验概率，联合选择FEC强度与未确认数据帧上限。基于ns-3模拟器实现，采用公开可验证的UALink 200G 1.0重传语义。

Result: 实验表明PREFACE提升有效吞吐量10.52%，降低P99延迟50.75%，减少重传47.52%，并改善环形AllReduce性能13.1-27.0%。

Conclusion: PREFACE通过预测性FEC控制有效缓解时间相关突发错误，在高带宽加速器互连中显著改善可靠性与性能，为大规模AI训练系统提供更高效的通信基础设施。

Abstract: Scale-up accelerator fabrics send latency-sensitive flits over serial links at hundreds of gigabits per second. Their reliability pipeline first relies on FEC, then detects residual failures and replays unacknowledged data. At these line rates, delayed feedback lets later flits enter the replay window before a residual failure is reported, so standard replay can amplify one corrupted flit into a suffix retransmission. This paper presents PREFACE, a pre-FEC controller for temporally correlated burst errors. A two-state Bayesian filter converts corrected-symbol observations into a next-flit burst posterior and jointly selects FEC strength with an outstanding-flit cap. We implement PREFACE in ns-3 with publicly verifiable UALink 200G 1.0 replay semantics. PREFACE improves goodput by 10.52%, lowers P99 latency by 50.75%, cuts replay by 47.52%, and improves modeled ring AllReduce by 13.1--27.0%.

</details>


### [242] [Completion-Path Credits: Multi-Resource Control for Scale-Up Fabrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17523)
*Fan Yang,Jiaqi Liu,Tao Jiang,Zhan Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: SemaCredit是一种接收器控制器，通过基于目标资源需求的向量式信用管理，显著改善小操作（Atomic、响应注入）的P99延迟，同时在HBM热点流量下保持与字节信用基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU和AI加速器互联的规模扩展架构需要承载张量传输、远程读/写、原子操作和通知等多种流量。传统基于字节的信用机制虽然能保护链路缓冲和HBM流式传输，但无法有效表征以小操作（Atomic执行、响应注入）为主的流量，导致资源竞争时小操作延迟恶化。

Method: 提出SemaCredit接收器控制器：每个远程内存操作根据目标资源需求向量进行准入，当对应的HBM、Atomic或响应阶段完成时返还各资源组件。在确定性事件模拟器中测试，包含多路径队列、八个HBM分区、串行Atomic引擎和响应引擎。

Result: 模拟结果显示：(1)HBM热点流量下与强字节基线匹配；(2)Atomic竞争下小操作P99延迟降低52.4%；(3)响应拥塞下小操作P99延迟降低10.2%；(4)AllReduce形态流量P99延迟改善57.7%；(5)远程读形态流量P99延迟改善14.5%；(6)HBM主导的MoE流量与字节信用性能一致。

Conclusion: SemaCredit通过精细化的资源级信用管理，在保持HBM密集型流量性能的前提下，显著优化了小操作的尾部延迟，解决了现有字节信用机制无法有效表征异构操作资源需求的局限性。

Abstract: Scale-up fabrics connecting GPUs and AI accelerators carry tensor transfers together with remote reads, writes, atomics, and notifications over shared target-side receiver resources. Byte-denominated credits protect link buffers and streaming HBM traffic, but poorly represent small operations dominated by Atomic execution or response injection. This paper presents SemaCredit, a receiver controller that admits each remote-memory operation against a vector of target-resource demands and returns each component when its corresponding HBM, Atomic, or response stage completes. In a deterministic event simulator with multipath queues, eight HBM partitions, a serialized Atomic engine, and a response engine, SemaCredit matches a strong per-resource byte baseline on HBM-hotspot traffic while reducing small-operation P99 latency by 52.4% under Atomic contention and 10.2% under response incast. Application-shaped mixes show 57.7% and 14.5% P99 latency improvements for AllReduce-shaped and remote-read-shaped traffic while matching byte credits on HBM-dominated MoE traffic.

</details>


### [243] [Array-Based Molecular Pulse Encoding for Neuro-Spike Communication in Intra-Body Nano-networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17675)
*Keyvan Aghababaiyan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经脉冲阵列的新型纳米机器通信方案，通过分子脉冲的特定排列方式编码信息，而非依赖精确时间同步，实现了75%-150%的通信速率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的神经脉冲通信依赖瞬时脉冲频率和时间间隔传递信息，这种时间编码方案需要发射器和接收器之间精确的时间同步。对于资源受限的纳米机器而言，精确时间同步是一个重大挑战。因此，未来体内纳米网络需要开发在降阶同步（如符号同步）条件下运行的通信方案。

Method: 提出了一种神经脉冲阵列通信方案，通过纳米机器发射的不同分子脉冲的特定排列来编码信息。该方法基于脉冲发射序列而非精确时间来区分符号，从而消除了对严格时间同步的需求。通过理论分析推导了符号间干扰概率、错误概率和可达通信速率的表达式。

Result: 分析和数值结果表明，所提出的阵列方案显著优于先前提出的符号同步模型，在不同扩散系数下实现了75%-150%的通信速率提升。理论推导的符号间干扰概率、错误概率和可达通信速率表达式验证了方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于神经脉冲阵列的通信方案成功解决了纳米机器通信中的时间同步难题，通过分子脉冲排列编码替代时间编码，大幅提升了通信性能，为体内纳米网络的可靠通信提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate a neuro-spike communication system designed to bridge severed connections between damaged neurons using auxiliary nano-machines. Natural neuro-spike communication typically relies on instantaneous spike rates and temporal intervals to convey information. However, these temporal encoding schemes require exact time synchronization between the transmitter and receiver, a requirement that poses a significant challenge for resource-constrained nano-machines. To address this issue, it is imperative for future intra-body nano-networks to develop communication schemes that operate under reduced-order synchronization (e.g., symbol-synchronized). In this paper, we propose a novel neuro-spike array-based communication scheme where information is encoded through the specific arrangement of distinct molecular pulses emitted by nano-machines. By distinguishing symbols based on the sequence of these emissions rather than their exact timing, the need for stringent time synchronization is eliminated. We theoretically analyze the performance of the proposed scheme by deriving expressions for the probability of inter-symbol interference (ISI), error probability, and the achievable communication rate. Analytical and numerical results demonstrate that our array-based scheme significantly outperforms previously proposed symbol-synchronized models, providing a 75% - 150% enhancement in the communication rate across various diffusion coefficients.

</details>


### [244] [Threat Aware Task Offloading and Caching for Secure UAV Assisted Vehicular Consumer Electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17794)
*Xiaoteng Yang,Sunil Prajapat,Zheng Lin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于无人机（UAV）辅助的车载边缘计算（VEC）协同架构，通过威胁感知任务卸载与智能时空缓存机制，在保障安全性的同时显著降低任务延迟并提升缓存效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载消费电子设备日益依赖计算密集且对时延敏感的服务，对VEC系统的效率、可靠性和安全性提出严格要求。在动态车载环境中，基于推理的信息泄露和异常通信行为进一步威胁系统性能与数据隐私。

Method: 设计了一种集成威胁感知任务卸载与跨路边单元（RSU）及UAV边缘节点的智能时空缓存的协同VEC架构。建立安全感知上行传输模型以捕获信息泄露风险和异常通信模式，实现自适应卸载决策。构建联合优化问题，在有限计算与存储资源下最小化端到端任务执行延迟并提升缓存利用率。提出威胁感知联合优化（TAGO）框架，结合近端策略优化（PPO）用于自适应任务卸载，以及基于Frank-Wolfe算法的梯度缓存更新以捕捉时空服务流行度。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法相比多种基线策略显著降低了任务延迟，提高了缓存效率，验证了其在安全高效UAV辅助车载消费电子设备系统中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该UAV辅助协同VEC架构通过威胁感知任务卸载与时空缓存机制，有效应对车载环境下的安全与性能挑战，实现了更低延迟和更高缓存利用率，为未来车载消费电子设备提供了可靠的边缘计算解决方案。

Abstract: Vehicular consumer electronics increasingly support computation-intensive and latency-sensitive services, imposing stringent efficiency, reliability, and security requirements on vehicular edge computing (VEC) systems. In dynamic vehicular environments, inference-based information leakage and anomalous communication behaviors further threaten system performance and data privacy. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a UAV-assisted cooperative VEC architecture that integrates threat-aware task offloading with intelligent spatiotemporal caching across roadside units (RSUs) and UAV edge nodes. A security-aware uplink transmission model is developed to capture potential information leakage risks and abnormal communication patterns, enabling adaptive offloading decisions. We formulate a joint optimization problem to minimize end-to-end task execution delay while improving cache utilization under limited computing and storage resources. To efficiently solve this problem, a Threat-Aware Joint Optimization (TAGO) framework is designed by combining proximal policy optimization for adaptive task offloading and a gradient-based caching update derived from the Frank-Wolfe algorithm to capture spatiotemporal service popularity. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly reduces task delay and improves cache efficiency compared with several baseline strategies, showing its effectiveness for secure and efficient UAV-assisted vehicular consumer electronics systems.

</details>

### [245] [Physically Consistent Channel Modeling and Signal Processing for Reconfigurable Wireless Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17122)
*Ahmad Dkhan,Simon Tarboush,Hadi Sarieddeen,Robert W. Heath,Hakan Bagci,Tareq Y. Al-Naffouri*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本教程提出了连接电磁学、电路与信息的统一框架，针对可重构天线系统的架构设计、信道建模及信号处理进行了深入分析，强调了物理结构对算法设计的重塑作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可重构天线虽能降低硬件复杂度与能耗，但其动态辐射特性和互耦效应挑战了传统信道模型的固定天线与解耦假设，亟需物理一致的建模框架。

Method: 建立连接麦克斯韦方程组、电路理论与信息论的框架，涵盖可重构架构分类、基于电磁学的信道噪声建模，以及架构感知信号处理算法研究。

Result: 构建了包含近场传播、互耦及电路损伤的端到端模型，并据此分析了信道估计、波束成形等算法的性能与复杂度权衡。

Conclusion: 本教程将物理架构、信道噪声模型及通信算法视为端到端设计的耦合组件，为物理一致的可重构无线系统提供了统一基础。

Abstract: Reconfigurable antennas are increasingly integrated into multi-antenna communication systems to exploit large apertures while reducing the hardware complexity, energy consumption, and implementation costs of classical massive arrays. Their reconfigurable electromagnetic (EM) properties, including dynamically varying radiation patterns and state-dependent mutual coupling, challenge the fixed-antenna and decoupled-port assumptions of conventional channel models. This motivates a physically consistent framework connecting Maxwell's equations, circuit theory, and information theory. In this tutorial, we develop a unified framework spanning three coupled dimensions: (i) reconfigurable antenna and transceiver architectures, (ii) physically consistent channel modeling, and (iii) physically consistent signal processing. We first establish a taxonomy covering tunable antennas, reconfigurable transceivers, and emerging array architectures, highlighting their reconfiguration mechanisms and hardware-performance trade-offs. We then develop modeling approaches based on Maxwell's equations, wavenumber-domain representations, multiport network theory, and computational electromagnetics, and use them to construct end-to-end channel and noise models that capture near-field propagation, mutual coupling, and circuit-level impairments. Building on these models, we examine architecture-aware channel estimation, beamforming, data detection, and channel decoding, emphasizing how physical structure reshapes algorithm design and performance-complexity trade-offs. Overall, the tutorial treats physical architecture, channel and noise models, and communication algorithms as coupled components of an end-to-end design, providing a unified foundation for physically consistent reconfigurable wireless systems.

</details>


### [246] [Multi-Tag Collision Recovery in UHF-RFID Using Self-Attention Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17216)
*Talha Akyildiz,Siva Aditya Gooty,Hessam Mahdavifar,Najme Ebrahimi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Passive ultra high frequency (UHF) radio frequency identification (RFID) enables battery-free tags to communicate with a reader through backscatter. When multiple tags respond in the same time slot, their waveforms overlap at the reader, and a conventional reader that follows framed slotted ALOHA (FSA) discards the resulting collided slot. This limits the throughput of the overall protocol even though the received signal still contains recoverable information about the responding tags. To address this limitation, we propose Self-Attention Tag Recovery (SATR), a transformer-based decoding algorithm that operates directly on the baseband in-phase and quadrature (I/Q) samples received during a standard tag response. SATR uses self-attention to model the temporal structure of the modulated waveform and learns candidate tag representations. It jointly estimates the number of responding tags and, more importantly, decodes the bit sequence of each detected tag. We numerically evaluate the decoding and throughput performance of SATR over a range of collision sizes and recovery configurations, and validate it with measurements of commercial UHF-RFID tags. The results show that, with proper design and training, SATR can reliably decode collisions of up to four tags. It achieves a throughput of approximately $0.815$ tags per slot under single acknowledgment and $1.87$ tags per slot under full recovery, corresponding to $2.2$ and $5.1$ times the conventional FSA limit of $1/e \approx 0.368$ tags per slot, while approaching optimal decoding performance and outperforming existing collision recovery methods.

</details>


### [247] [Task-Based Evaluation of Raw Radar Data Compression: A Pre-Registered Study of Where Classical Codecs Fail to Preserve Target Detection, and Why](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17269)
*Eric Michael Chrabot*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于任务评估的SAR原始雷达压缩方法，该方法不再依赖传统的图像质量指标（PSNR、SSIM、SQNR），而是在SAR聚焦后基于双侧重检测判据（CFAR检测一致性下限和匹配阈值下的虚警预算）对编解码器进行评估。在Sentinel-1和AFRL Gotcha GMTI数据上的实验表明，3位BAQ可在6.12比特/复采样率下维持实用性，传统配置在低于4.86比特/复采样率时无法维持实用性，且每个失败模式都有可识别的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成孔径雷达（SAR）系统以超过下行链路容量的速率采集原始I/Q回波数据，现役系统采用块自适应量化（BAQ/FDBAQ）进行机载压缩。提出的替代方案（包括学习型压缩）通常使用图像质量指标（PSNR、SSIM、SQNR）进行评估，但这些指标无法衡量数据是否仍支持其操作性用途。因此，需要一种新的评估方法，直接衡量压缩后的数据是否仍能支持雷达检测任务。

Method: 作者提出了一种预注册的、基于任务评估的原始雷达压缩方法：在SAR聚焦后，使用在未经压缩数据上一次训练并冻结的任务模型，基于双侧重检测判据（CFAR检测一致性下限和匹配阈值下的虚警预算）对编解码器进行评分。该方法还发布了一个评估工具包，包含具有验证往返可逆性的单值去调/聚焦变换（相对误差约1e-7）以及完整的预注册跟踪记录。

Result: 在Sentinel-1条带级Level-0数据上，评估重现了现役FDBAQ编解码器的工作点（3位BAQ在6.12比特/复采样率下维持实用性），并对之前报告的重新构建晶格伪影进行了测试和排除。没有传统配置在该场景下能以低于4.86比特/复采样率（约13:1）维持实用性，每个失败都有可识别的机制：原始回波没有变换编码增益，而聚焦后的能量集中带来了预测的检测增益（Pd 0.888 vs 0.691，2比特/复采样率），但通过小波振铃将其转化为虚警；仅检测概率就会认证产生约53倍可容忍虚警计数的编解码器。将相同方案应用于AFRL Gotcha GMTI数据（机载，未机载压缩）独立复制了两个发现（前沿在7.99比特/复采样率）。研究还发布了一个评估工具包和完整的预注册跟踪记录，包括一个撤回的过度声明和针对小型学习型编解码器的有界负结果。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于任务评估的SAR压缩方法提供了一个比传统图像质量指标更有意义的评估框架，能够直接衡量压缩数据对雷达检测任务的实用性。实验结果表明，传统编解码器在压缩率低于约13:1时失去实用性，且失败模式可归因于特定机制（如小波振铃导致的虚警）。该方法已被验证可推广至不同数据集，为未来的雷达压缩研究提供了可复现的评估标准。

Abstract: Synthetic aperture radar (SAR) systems collect raw I/Q echo data at rates that exceed downlink capacity; fielded systems compress onboard with block-adaptive quantization (BAQ/FDBAQ). Proposed replacements, including learned compression, are typically evaluated with image-quality metrics (PSNR, SSIM, SQNR); none measures whether the data still supports its operational use. We introduce a pre-registered, task-based evaluation methodology for raw radar compression: codecs are scored after SAR focusing against a two-sided detection criterion -- a CFAR detection-agreement floor and a false-alarm budget at matched threshold -- using frozen task models trained once on uncompressed data. On Sentinel-1 stripmap Level-0 data the evaluation reproduces the operating point of the fielded FDBAQ codec (3-bit BAQ sustains utility at 6.12 bits per complex sample), with a previously reported reconstruction-lattice artifact tested for and ruled out. No classical configuration tested sustains utility below 4.86 bits per complex sample (roughly 13:1) on this scene, and each failure has an identifiable mechanism: raw echoes offer no transform-coding gain, while energy concentration after focusing delivers the predicted detection gain (Pd 0.888 vs 0.691 at 2 bits per complex sample) but converts it into false alarms through wavelet ringing; detection probability alone would have certified a codec producing roughly 53 times the tolerable false-alarm count. Applying the same protocol to AFRL Gotcha GMTI data (airborne, never compressed onboard) replicates both findings independently (frontier at 7.99 bits per complex sample). We release the evaluation harness, a unitary dechirp/focus transform with verified round-trip invertibility (relative error ~1e-7), and the complete pre-registration trail, including one retracted overclaim and a bounded negative result for a small learned codec, reported as a lower bound.

</details>


### [248] [Channel Knowledge Map Enabled Low-Complexity Dynamic Radio Environment Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17322)
*Yujun Lin,Zhiqiang Xiao,Hao Wu,Xiaoqiang Qiao,Fayu Wan,Tao Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信道知识图（CKM）的动态无线电环境重建方法，通过利用解耦于发射端辐射特征的可复用路径级传播知识，在发射机配置动态变化时实现高效、无需新测量的无线电地图更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在发射机配置高度动态的无线环境中，准确的实时无线电环境重建具有重要意义但极具挑战性。传统方法如压缩感知（CS）、克里金方法和U-Net通常需要环境测量和重建开销来更新无线电环境，当发射机位置或辐射图案发生变化时，这些方法效率较低。

Method: 提出一种新型信道知识图（CKM）启用的动态无线电环境重建方法。CKM能够存储可复用的路径级传播知识，这些知识与发射端辐射特性解耦。在已知发射机位置和辐射图案的情况下，利用CKM进行轻量级前向无线电地图生成，无需新的目标地图测量。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法在重建精度上优于CS、Kriging和U-Net，并且在动态发射机配置下展现出强大的鲁棒性性能。

Conclusion: 所提方法证明了在动态无线网络中进行灵活、高效无线电环境重建的潜力，为应对发射机配置动态变化场景提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate and timely radio environment reconstruction is important but challenging under particularly dynamic transmitter configurations. The conventional methods such as compressed sensing (CS), Kriging method or U-Net typically require environment measurements and reconstruction overhead for radio environment updating as the transmitter locations or radiation patterns change. In this paper, we propose a novel channel knowledge map (CKM)-enabled dynamic radio environment reconstruction method for efficient radio map updating. Specifically, the recently proposed CKM can store reusable path-level propagation knowledge that is decoupled from the transmitter-side radiation characteristics. We can leverage CKM for lightweight forward radio map generation as the transmitter locations and radiation patterns are known, without requiring new target-map measurements. Simulation results show that the proposed method outperforms CS, Kriging, and U-Net in reconstruction accuracy and exhibits strong robustness performance under dynamic transmitter configurations, which demonstrates the potential of the proposed method for flexible and efficient radio environment reconstruction in dynamic wireless networks.

</details>


### [249] [Finite-range Lattice Momentum Operators for Quantum Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17327)
*Jan C Olivier,Etienne Barnard*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Z变换的框架分析晶格量子场论中有限程动量算符，将费米子加倍问题重新表述为离散动量算符在单位圆上存在非期望零点的混叠现象。证明不存在满足所有鬼态抑制条件的有理函数，据此发展出通过频域最小二乘近似构造的有限冲激响应(FIR)动量算符方法。该方法无需添加破坏对称性的Wilson项或无限程SLAC导数，仅依靠谱近似精度实现鬼态抑制，数值验证表明在θ≈π区域仅存在相干平面波且群速度超光速，无鬼态波包解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对晶格量子场论中长期存在的费米子加倍问题，现有解决方案（Wilson项导致对称性破缺、SLAC导数需无限程）存在根本性局限。需要一种既能保持规范对称性又能有效抑制鬼态的有限程动量算符设计方案。

Method: 将平移不变晶格算符表示为单位圆上复变量z的函数，运用数字信号处理工具和有理逼近理论分析其谱性质。将费米子加倍问题重构为离散动量算符在单位圆上的零点结构约束问题，并证明任何有理传递函数均无法满足全部鬼态抑制条件。基于此发展FIR方法：在频域求解最小二乘近似问题，构造在完整布里渊区内逼近连续导数的有限程动量算符。

Result: 理论证明揭示了有理函数无法同时满足鬼态抑制条件；所提FIR算符通过谱近似精度而非对称性破缺实现鬼态抑制；数值模拟证实θ≈π区域仅存在相干平面波传播，其群速度远超光速但属于非物理激发，且不存在鬼态波包解。

Conclusion: 本框架成功将鬼态抑制问题转化为频域零点分布约束，FIR方法以有限程操作实现了传统Wilson/SLAC方法难以兼顾的精确谱近似与鬼态消除，为晶格QFT中的动量算符设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: We propose a Z-transform framework for the analysis and synthesis of finite range lattice momentum operators in quantum field theory. In this formulation, translation-invariant lattice operators are represented as functions of the complex variable $z$ in the unit circle, allowing their spectral properties to be analyzed using tools from digital signal processing and rational approximation theory.
  Within this framework, the fermion doubling problem is reinterpreted as the appearance of unwanted zeros of the discrete momentum operator on the unit circle --- an aliasing phenomenon in the sense of the Nyquist sampling theorem --- and the conditions for ghost suppression are expressed as precise constraints on the zero structure of the operator's transfer function. It is proven that no rational function can satisfy all required conditions simultaneously, motivating the finite impulse response approach developed here.
  This reframing naturally suggests a class of finite-range momentum operators, constructed by solving a least-squares approximation problem in the frequency domain. The resulting finite impulse response (FIR) operator approximates the continuum derivative across the full Brillouin zone, with ghost suppression achieved through the accuracy of the spectral approximation rather than through the addition of a symmetry-breaking Wilson term or the infinite-range nonlocal SLAC derivative. Numerical investigation confirms that near $θ= π$ only plane waves propagate coherently, and these exhibit group velocities far exceeding the speed of light, further distinguishing them from physical low-energy excitations. No ghost wave packet solutions exist near $θ= π$.

</details>


### [250] [Multi-Sensor Edge Angle Detection for Performance Analysis in Ski Jumping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17448)
*Ivan Simeonov,Lukas Schulthess,Hanna Mueller,Marc Nölke,Michele Magno,Luca Benini,Christoph Leitner*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种集成超声波时间飞行（ToF）传感器与惯性测量单元（IMU）的可穿戴式测量系统，用于在不改变滑雪板表面的情况下实时、精确地测量滑雪跳跃运动员的板边角和靴子倾斜角，解决现有多系统方案带来的质量分布变化和平衡干扰问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在滑雪跳跃的滑翔阶段，空气动力学姿态（尤其是板边角）对升阻比和飞行距离至关重要。现有测量方案需要同时在靴子和滑雪板上安装传感器，导致质量分布改变、平衡受影响以及系统复杂度增加，限制了其在训练和比赛中的实用价值。

Method: 设计并实现了一个仅安装在靴子上的单一测量单元，集成两个超声波ToF传感器和一个鞋内IMU。通过ToF测距和IMU姿态数据，结合靴板几何关系进行重构，从而间接推算板边角。实验室环境下与参考测量进行对比验证。

Result: 实验显示系统角度分辨率为0.4500°，平均绝对误差（MAE）为0.2640°，决定系数（R²）超过99%，表明与参考数据高度线性一致。端到端延迟30.31 ms，功耗1.28 mW，整机重量仅18.6 g，满足实时反馈和低干扰需求。

Conclusion: 该穿戴式系统能够在不改变滑雪板结构的前提下，实现高精度、低延迟、低功耗的板边角和靴子姿态测量，为运动员训练提供实时反馈，具有良好的应用前景和可扩展性。

Abstract: In ski jumping, performance during the gliding phase depends on achieving an aerodynamic posture that maximizes the lift-to-drag ratio. In the V-style technique, the ski edge angle is a key determinant. Reducing the edge angle flattens the skis, increases their effective surface area, and improves aerodynamic lift, ultimately contributing to longer flight distances. Ski edge angles are biomechanically constrained by the limited range of ankle inversion. Current sensing solutions widely quantify these angles using multi-system approaches that combine sensor signals through geometric relations. Such configurations require instrumentation on both the boot and the ski, altering mass distribution, affecting balance during flight, and increasing system complexity. To overcome these limitations, this work presents a wearable sensing system that measures both boot inclination and ski edge angle without modifying the ski surface. Two ultrasonic Time of Flight (ToF) sensors and an in-shoe Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) are integrated into a single boot-mounted unit. Edge angles are estimated by combining ultrasonic distance measurements with IMU data through geometric reconstruction of the boot-ski configuration. Laboratory experiments demonstrate an angle resolution of 0.4500°, a Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 0.2640°, and a coefficient of determination exceeding 99\% when compared with reference measurements, indicating strong linear agreement between the two modalities. The system achieves an end-to-end latency of 30.31 ms, enabling real-time feedback suitable for athlete training, while consuming 1.28 mW of power. With a total weight of only 18.6 g the proposed system enables unobtrusive measurement of ski edge angle and boot orientation.

</details>


### [251] [Super-resolution ranging using a sub-terahertz self-injection-locked frequency-modulated radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17476)
*Hossein Naghavi,Zainulabideen Khalifa,Hamad Alotaibi,Farzad Khoeini,James Gruber,Morteza Tavakoli Taba,Aditya Varma Muppala,Saghar Adler,Ali Mostajeran,Mohammed Aseeri,Andreia Cathelin,Ehsan Afshari*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自注入锁定（SIL）的全集成亚太赫兹自调谐频率调制（AFM）雷达，通过形成中间频率梳状光谱实现超宽带扫描，克服了传统FMCW雷达带宽窄和相位噪声大的问题，实现了亚毫米级距离分辨率和<0.002%的距离精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有亚太赫兹/太赫兹FMCW雷达中，光子方法虽然性能强但缺乏集成性和便携性；电子集成雷达带宽窄（<100 GHz），距离分辨率仅毫米级（>1.5 mm），且宽带宽导致相位噪声增加，影响距离测量精度。需要一种兼具高集成度、宽带宽和低相位噪声的雷达方案。

Method: 提出基于SIL机制的AFM雷达，通过在二次接收机中形成中间频率梳状光谱，实现超宽带（延伸至太赫兹扫描范围）和降低发射机相位噪声，从而突破传统FMCW雷达的限制。

Result: 理论分析和实验验证表明，该雷达实现了亚毫米级距离分辨率，距离精度<0.002%，并能对带有微米特征的覆盖印刷字母进行成像。

Conclusion: 基于SIL的AFM雷达克服了FMCW雷达的带宽和相位噪声挑战，为亚太赫兹/太赫兹成像应用提供了高分辨率和高精度的距离测量能力。

Abstract: Sub-terahertz (sub-THz) and terahertz (THz) frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radars have opened a plethora of scientific and industrial applications, especially in the imaging field. While strong candidates for sub-THz/THz FMCW radar imagers are implemented using photonic methods, there is a desire to achieve the full integration and portability that only electronics can offer. However, integrated electronic sub-THz/THz FMCW radars have significantly lower bandwidth (< 100 GHz) than photonic-based radars, restricting the radar range resolution to the millimeter scale (> 1.5 mm). In addition, the electronic FMCW radar's broad bandwidth comes with increased transmitter phase noise, consequently degrading the radar range accuracy. Here, we present a sub-THz fully-integrated autodyne frequency-modulated (AFM) radar utilizing a self-injection locking (SIL) mechanism that fundamentally overcomes the aforementioned challenges of FMCW radars. The AFM radar supports an exceptionally wide effective bandwidth extending into the terahertz sweep range by forming an intermediate frequency comb spectrum in a quadratic receiver, unlocking the path for super-resolution ranging. Furthermore, SIL significantly reduces the transmitter's phase noise, allowing high-accuracy range measurements. We theoretically describe and experimentally demonstrate the SIL operation of the AFM radar. The proposed radar experimentally achieves sub-millimeter range resolution and a range accuracy of < 0.002%, enabling the imaging of covered printed letters with micrometer features.

</details>


### [252] [Channel Modeling for Phase-Based Ranging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17497)
*Till Droemmer,Markus Gardill*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Python的蓝牙信道探测物理层仿真框架，实现了符合蓝牙核心规范v6.2的Mode 3相位测距功能，支持多种传播条件和硬件损伤效应建模，为蓝牙测距和定位算法研究提供基础平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高层仿真工具无法充分表示物理层效应，而空中实验难以分离分析这些效应。研究者需要能够进行可控、可重复分析的物理层仿真环境，以便深入研究蓝牙信道探测的测距性能。

Method: 构建Python仿真框架，实现2.4GHz ISM频段72个活跃音的Mode 3相位测距；模拟自由空间、静态多径和IEEE 802.15.4a随机多径信道；建模相位噪声、相位斜坡、载波频率偏移、IQ不平衡、ADC量化和Wi-Fi窄带干扰等硬件效应；参数来源于商用SoC数据手册和现场测量。

Result: 开发出能够生成物理层信号、建模信道并注入损伤效应的完整仿真框架，可准确模拟蓝牙信道探测中的各种实际影响因子，为后续算法验证提供可靠测试环境。

Conclusion: 该仿真框架专注于物理层信号生成、信道建模和损伤注入，弥补了现有工具在物理层细节建模方面的不足，为未来蓝牙信道探测测距和定位算法研究奠定了重要的技术基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a Python-based simulation framework for the physical layer of Bluetooth Channel Sounding in accordance with Bluetooth Core Specification v6.2. The simulator implements Mode 3 phase-based ranging across 72 active tones in the 2.4 GHz ISM band and supports multiple propagation conditions, including free-space, static multipath, and IEEE 802.15.4a stochastic multipath channels. In addition, it models relevant hardware and interference effects such as phase noise, phase ramp, carrier frequency offset, IQ imbalance, ADC quantization, and narrowband interference from co-located Wi-Fi systems, with parameters derived from commercial SoC datasheets and field measurements. The framework enables controlled and repeatable analysis of physical layer effects that are difficult to isolate in over-the-air experiments and are not sufficiently represented in existing higher-level simulation tools. This work focuses exclusively on physical layer signal generation, channel modeling, and impairment injection, providing a foundation for future studies on Bluetooth Channel Sounding ranging and localization algorithms.

</details>


### [253] [Channel2World: A Wireless Foundation Model for RF Environment Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17544)
*Hyung-Joo Moon,Joonkyu Jang,Kwang Soon Kim,Seong-Lyun Kim,Robert W. Heath,Chan-Byoung Chae*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出Channel2World无线基础模型，通过Transformer编码器从MIMO信道-位置数据中学习可复用的环境级表示，预训练后作为通用环境条件模块用于下游任务，在未见环境中实现定位、CSI重建和几何重构，性能优于或媲美站点特定微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无线信道处理将信道视为链路特定观测，忽略了多径结构由周围射频环境决定的事实，导致难以泛化到新环境且需站点特定微调。本文旨在学习可复用的环境级表示，以实现跨部署的泛化能力。

Method: 提出Channel2World模型，使用基于Transformer的编码器将同一基站环境内的多天线信道聚合为无线世界嵌入。通过上下文查询预测进行预训练，即利用上下文信道预测不相交查询信道的UE位置和相对路径增益。预训练后编码器冻结，作为任务无关的环境条件模块用于下游模型。使用26,000个环境的射线追踪数据，每个环境约5,000个信道测量进行预训练。

Result: 在UE定位、波束域CSI重建和射频可观测几何重构任务上评估，表明学习到的嵌入在未见环境中提供有效的条件信息。对于定位和CSI重建，基于嵌入的条件信息优于或媲美站点特定微调，而微调需要任务特定标注数据和额外的梯度自适应。嵌入还支持主导反射结构的重构。

Conclusion: 学习到的环境级潜在空间能够跨部署泛化，使模型无需站点特定微调即可适应未见环境，并作为可复用的环境先验用于多种任务，证明了其作为通用无线基础模型的潜力。

Abstract: Wireless channels are commonly treated as link-specific observations, although their multipath structure is governed by the surrounding radio-frequency (RF) environment. In this paper, we propose Channel2World, a wireless foundation model that learns a reusable environment-level representation from multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) channel-position observations. The model aggregates channels collected within the same base-station-centered environment into a wireless world embedding using a Transformer-based encoder. The encoder is pretrained through context-query prediction, where context channels condition user equipment (UE) position and relative path-gain prediction for disjoint query channels. After pretraining, the encoder is frozen and used as a task-agnostic environment-conditioning module for downstream wireless models, enabling adaptation to unseen environments without site-specific fine-tuning. To learn an environment-level latent space that generalizes across deployments, we pretrain Channel2World using ray-tracing data from 26,000 environments, with approximately 5,000 channel measurements per environment. Evaluations on UE localization, beam-domain channel state information (CSI) reconstruction, and RF-observable geometry reconstruction show that the learned embeddings provide effective conditioning in unseen environments. For localization and CSI reconstruction tasks, embedding-based conditioning outperforms or remains competitive with site-specific fine-tuning, although fine-tuning requires task-specific labeled data and additional gradient-based adaptation. The embeddings also support the reconstruction of dominant reflector structures, indicating their utility as reusable environmental priors across tasks.

</details>


### [254] [Geometry-Aware DRL for Multi-Subband Scheduling in Satellite-Assisted UAM Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17612)
*Hyung-Joo Moon,Sangha Park,Chan-Byoung Chae,Robert W. Heath*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoSetPPO方法，一种基于几何感知的集合注意力近端策略优化算法，用于解决城市空中交通(UAM)在天地一体化协作网络中的下行调度问题，实验显示该方法在收敛性、回报值、调度可行性及延迟方面均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着城市空中交通(UAM)的发展，天地一体化协作网络中的下行调度面临挑战：多个地面站(GS)采用窄三维波束并共享多个子带频谱，卫星提供正交频段服务。快速时变几何结构和方向性干扰要求对基站关联、GS子带分配和发射功率进行联合决策。

Method: 1) 问题建模：构建有限时域混合离散-连续优化问题，以最大化系统总速率为目标，同时惩罚切换次数和GS过载，仅利用UAM位置和速度信息；2) GeoSetPPO方法：设计几何感知的集合注意力近端策略优化(PPO)方法，输出每个UAM的离散关联和子带决策，采用置换不变性表示；3) 功率分配：在每个调度条件下，通过逐时隙逐次凸逼近(SCA)模块在功率预算和最小信干噪比(SINR)约束下计算GS功率；4) 两阶段训练策略：将奖励评估从均匀功率过渡到基于SCA的功率分配，以降低训练成本并提高稳定性。

Result: 仿真结果展示：1) 稳定收敛特性；2) 在所考虑训练设置下，回报值高于多层感知器(MLP)和Transformer-based PPO方法；3) 相对于算法驱动和距离驱动的调度器，在奖励和调度可行性方面表现优异；4) 在更大规模评估网络中，相比之前的算法驱动方法，将调度延迟从40.84ms降低至2.90ms。

Conclusion: GeoSetPPO方法有效解决了UAM天地一体化网络中的组合调度问题，通过几何感知和集合注意力机制实现高效的离散决策，结合SCA功率分配和两阶段训练策略，在性能指标和计算效率上均显著优于现有方法，为大规模UAM网络的实时调度提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate downlink scheduling for urban air mobility (UAM) in a cooperative space-air-ground integrated network. Multiple ground stations (GSs) employ narrow three-dimensional beams and share spectrum across multiple subbands, while a satellite provides an orthogonal-band service option. Rapidly time-varying geometry and directional interference require joint decisions on base station association, GS subband assignment, and transmit powers. We formulate a finite-horizon mixed discrete-continuous problem that maximizes sum rate while penalizing handovers and GS overload, using only UAM positions and velocities. To address the combinatorial scheduling problem, we propose GeoSetPPO, a geometry-aware set-attention proximal policy optimization (PPO) method that outputs per-UAM discrete association and subband decisions with permutation-invariant representations. Conditioned on each schedule, GS powers are computed by a per-slot successive convex approximation (SCA) module under per-GS power budgets and minimum signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) constraints. To reduce training cost and improve stability, we adopt a two-stage training strategy that transitions reward evaluation from uniform power to SCA-based power allocation. Simulations demonstrate stable convergence, higher returns than multi-layer perceptron (MLP)- and Transformer-based PPO under the considered training setting, and favorable reward and schedule-feasibility performance relative to algorithm-based and distance-based schedulers. In the larger evaluated network, GeoSetPPO also reduces the scheduling latency from 40.84 ms to 2.90 ms relative to the previous algorithm-based method.

</details>


### [255] [On the Probability of Network States with Gaussian Connectivity Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17622)
*Amy S. Inwood,Peter J. Smith,Pawel Dmochowski,Carl P. Dettmann,Justin P. Coon,Michail Matthaiou*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究三维空间中N个移动设备随机网络的连通性，节点位置服从高斯分布，连通概率由节点间距离的高斯函数决定，通过图拉普拉斯矩阵分析网络状态概率，推导小网络、完整网络和孤立节点的连通性概率，并推广到更大网络的近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无线传感器网络、车联网等移动设备网络的发展，研究三维随机网络的连通性对于网络设计、资源分配和可靠性分析具有重要意义。

Method: 利用节点位置的高斯分布和基于高斯连接函数的连接概率，推导网络状态概率的解析表达式，基于图拉普拉斯矩阵，计算小网络、完整网络和孤立节点的连通性概率，并对特殊情况简化。

Result: 得到了小网络连通性概率、完整网络概率和孤立节点概率的解析结果，并给出了大网络连通性概率的近似公式。

Conclusion: 通过图拉普拉斯矩阵方法，成功分析了三维高斯随机网络的连通性特性，为移动设备网络的可靠性评估提供了理论工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the connectivity of a random network of N mobile devices in three dimensions (3D), where the location of each device or node has a Gaussian distribution in each dimension. For each pair of nodes, the probability of connectivity is related to the nodes' separation by a Gaussian connectivity function. The fundamental analytical tool for studying such systems is the probability of a given network state, derived and expressed in terms of its graph Laplacian. Leveraging this result, we obtain results for the connectivity of small networks, the probability of a complete network (where all nodes are connected to all other nodes), and the probability of an isolated node, which gives an approximation to the connectivity probability of larger networks. The general results are then simplified for special cases and limiting scenarios.

</details>


### [256] [Empirical mode decomposition and interpretable machine learning for preterm birth classification from electrohysterography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17643)
*Umesha Tilakarathna,Senith Jayakody,Kalana Jayasooriya,Roshan Godaliyadda,Parakrama Ekanayake,Isuru Nawinne,Chathura Rathnayake*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Electrohysterography (EHG) combined with empirical mode decomposition (EMD), specifically using the first intrinsic mode function (IMF1), enables effective non‑invasive classification of term versus preterm pregnancies. Fixed‑3‑minute IMF1 features processed with a Random Forest classifier achieved high accuracy (0.8308) and outperformed time‑domain features, suggesting promising potential for preterm birth risk assessment pending validation in larger cohorts.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Preterm birth (PTB) is a major global health challenge, and reliable non‑invasive risk assessment remains elusive. Electrohysterography (EHG) offers a potential solution by recording uterine electrical activity, but its performance can be overstated when data from the same recording are split across training and validation sets. This study aims to evaluate whether empirical mode decomposition (EMD) applied to EHG signals can robustly discriminate term from preterm pregnancies.

Method: The study used 26 pregnancy recordings (13 preterm, 13 term) from the public TPEHGT dataset. Two segmentation strategies—annotated intervals and non‑overlapping fixed 3‑minute windows—were compared. The first four intrinsic mode functions (IMFs) derived from EMD were examined. Fourteen features were extracted from each of three EHG channels and evaluated with nine classifiers using repeated five‑fold recording‑grouped cross‑validation and recording‑level aggregation.

Result: IMF1 yielded the strongest mean classification performance. With fixed 3‑minute IMF1 features, Random Forest achieved mean accuracy of 0.8308, F1‑score of 0.7969, balanced accuracy of 0.8308, Matthews correlation coefficient of 0.6998, ROC‑AUC of 0.8157, and average precision of 0.8877. IMF1 also outperformed matched filtered time‑domain features across all reported metrics. Preterm recordings exhibited smaller, more regularly spaced peak‑like events, lower temporal‑energy measures, and higher entropy compared to term recordings.

Conclusion: The findings support further investigation of IMF1‑based EHG classification for preterm birth prediction in larger, independent cohorts, as the approach demonstrates promising discriminative capability while avoiding data‑leakage issues.

Abstract: Preterm birth (PTB) remains a major global health problem, and reliable non-invasive risk assessment remains difficult. Electrohysterography (EHG) records uterine electrical activity from the maternal abdomen and may support PTB assessment, but performance can be inflated when segments from the same recording are split across training and validation folds. We evaluated empirical mode decomposition (EMD) for term-versus-preterm classification using 26 pregnancy recordings (13 preterm, 13 term) from the public TPEHGT dataset. Annotated intervals and non-overlapping fixed 3-minute windows were compared, and the first four intrinsic mode functions (IMFs) were evaluated. Fourteen features from each of three EHG channels were assessed with nine classifiers using repeated five-fold recording-grouped cross-validation and recording-level aggregation. IMF1 gave the strongest mean performance. With fixed 3-minute IMF1 features, Random Forest achieved mean accuracy 0.8308, F1 0.7969, balanced accuracy 0.8308, MCC 0.6998, ROC-AUC 0.8157, and average precision 0.8877. IMF1 also outperformed matched filtered time-domain features across all reported mean metrics. Preterm recordings showed smaller, more regularly spaced peak-like events, lower temporal-energy measures, and higher entropy. These findings support further evaluation of IMF1-based EHG classification in larger independent cohorts.

</details>


### [257] [Statistical Characterization and Block-EM Estimation of Frequency-Domain NSI for OFDM Systems in Bursty Impulsive Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17683)
*Chin-Hung Chen,Wim van Houtum,Yan Wu,Alex Alvarado*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对OFDM系统中的冲激噪声，本文提出一种频率域、基于分块的抑制框架，通过高斯混合模型与EM算法实现噪声状态估计，在不破坏子载波正交性的前提下提升性能并降低复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冲激噪声具有高功率和非高斯特性，严重干扰正交频分复用（OFDM）系统。现有时域抑制方法在离散傅里叶变换（DFT）前处理，增加复杂度、与OFDM频率域流程不匹配，且可能因不完美的噪声减除破坏子载波正交性。因此需要在频率域直接处理冲激噪声，保持OFDM系统完整性并降低复杂度。

Method: 首先利用变换后的高斯混合模型推导冲激噪声在频率域的统计表示；基于该模型设计理想噪声状态信息（NSI）下的最优接收机；提出无监督的基于分块的EM框架用于NSI估计，并实现三种变体：符号级方差更新EM、序列级转移更新EM以及利用稀疏性先验的MAP型EM。

Result: 所提频率域方案在DFT之后操作，无缝集成到OFDM处理链中，保持子载波正交性，并利用已知的冲激噪声分块结构获得显著性能提升，无需时域冲激重建的高复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种频率域、基于分块的冲激噪声抑制方法，通过高斯混合模型与EM算法实现噪声状态估计，在不破坏子载波正交性的前提下显著提升OFDM系统性能，且避免了时域方法的高复杂度。

Abstract: Impulsive noise (IN), characterized by its high power and non-Gaussian distribution, poses a critical challenge in modern orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) systems, driven by the proliferation of electronic devices. Current IN mitigation techniques rely heavily on time-domain processing. These methods apply before the discrete Fourier transform (DFT), introducing additional complexity, failing to align with OFDM's inherent frequency-domain processing flow, and risking the destruction of subcarrier orthogonality due to imperfect IN subtraction. To address these limitations, we propose a frequency-domain, block-based framework for mitigating IN. The statistical representation of IN in the frequency domain is first derived using a transformed Gaussian mixture model. Based on this model, we develop an optimal receiver that leverages perfect noise state information (NSI), thereby identifying scenarios in which NSI is critical. We then propose an unsupervised block-based expectation-maximization (EM) framework for NSI estimation and develop three variants for evaluation. These include a simple symbol-by-symbol variance-updated EM, a sequence-based transition-updated EM, and a MAP-based EM that exploits a sparsity-promoting prior to automatically prune the number of states. Our frequency-domain design operates after the DFT, seamlessly integrates with the OFDM processing chain, preserves subcarrier orthogonality, and leverages the known IN block structure to achieve substantial performance gains without the immense complexity of time-domain impulse reconstruction.

</details>


### [258] [M-QAM MIMO Maximum-Likelihood Detection with QAOA: ML-Rate Offline Angle Design and Correlated Infinite-Size Spin-Glass Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17721)
*Burhan Gülbahar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文通过开发相关多物种自旋玻璃框架，解决M-QAM中QAOA角度设计问题，首次利用ML率进行优化，实现M-QAM检测可扩展规模基准，仿真验证在4096-QAM、128天线规模下性能显著优于现有方法，为容错量子电路上的近最优解码奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子近似优化算法(QAOA)应用于多输入多输出(MIMO)系统中的NP困难最大似然(ML)检测问题。现有M-QAM检测器通过预期Ising能量设计角度（逐实例在线、预热启动或渐进式），但一次性训练设计仅限于B/QPSK或块局部，M-QAM缺乏可扩展规模基准。无限规模自旋玻璃理论假设独立无序性，匹配B/QPSK的保留协方差，但不适用于M-QAM的相关耦合和场。

Method: 提出相关无限规模多物种自旋玻璃框架，协方差匹配评估器使能量成为离线目标，提供可扩展规模基准。首次利用ML率（采样ML字符串的指数率）进行MIMO检测中的QAOA角度设计。能量评估器在O(p 4^p)成本下实现q-free，ML率将角度从固定的q_ref-qubit参考转移。

Result: 测试达到4096-QAM、128天线、p=30、每符号SNR 0-45 dB。仿真中ML率按幂律r_0 p^{-α}下降，采样设计指数更大，在5×5 16-QAM (0-20 dB)和3×3 64-QAM (8-28 dB)中跟踪精确ML，误码率(BER)优势随SNR扩大至两个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法指向更深的无噪声容错量子(FTQ)电路上实现近最优解码。

Abstract: The quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) targets NP-hard maximum-likelihood (ML) detection in multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems. Existing $M$-ary quadrature amplitude modulation (M-QAM) detectors design angles by expected Ising energy: online per instance, warm-started, or ramped, while train-once designs remain B/QPSK-only or block-local, leaving M-QAM without a size-scalable benchmark. Their infinite-size spin-glass theory assumes independent disorder, matching the retained covariances at B/QPSK but not M-QAM's correlated couplings and fields. We develop a correlated infinite-size multi-species spin-glass framework whose covariance-matched evaluators make that energy an offline objective with a size-scalable benchmark. In addition, the ML rate, the exponential rate of sampling the ML string, is for the first time exploited for QAOA angle design in MIMO detection. The energy evaluator is $q$-free at $O(p\,4^p)$ cost while the ML rate transfers angles from a fixed $q_{\rm ref}$-qubit reference. Tests reach 4096-QAM, 128 antennas, $p=30$ and per-symbol SNR 0-45 dB. In simulations, ML rates fall as a power law $r_0\,p^{-α}$, with larger exponents for the sampling design, which tracks exact ML at $5\times5$ 16-QAM (0-20 dB) and $3\times3$ 64-QAM (8-28 dB) while its bit-error rate (BER) advantage widens with SNR to two orders of magnitude. The approach points toward near-optimum decoding on deeper noiseless fault-tolerant quantum (FTQ) circuits.

</details>


### [259] [Electromagnetic World Model for 6G: A Unified Framework for Joint Environment Reconstruction and Channel Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17769)
*Yizhu Zhao,Li Yu,Jianhua Zhang,Yuxiang Zhang,Zhen Zhang,Guangyi Liu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出电磁世界模型(EMWM)，首个用于6G系统联合环境重建和信道预测的统一框架。该模型通过编码部分CSI和多视角RGB图像，学习通用电磁表示，可实现完整CSI重建和3D点云生成。实验证明其在CSI预测和环境重建方面均优于传统神经网络和大语言模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G无线系统中感知、通信和智能的集成成为关键使能技术，智能终端需要同时支持高效链路建立和可靠环境感知。然而现有研究主要利用感知或通信信息解决单一任务（如信道预测或环境重建），缺乏统一的联合处理框架。

Method: EMWM基于光学和射频信号对周围环境的共同依赖性，提出统一框架。具体方法包括：1）将部分CSI和多视角RGB图像编码为CSI和视觉token；2）使用具有局部和全局聚合的分层世界模型主干联合处理；3）基于混合专家(MoE)的CSI预测头重建完整CSI；4）深度预测头估计多视角深度图并转换为3D点云；5）基于校园数字孪生构建大规模多模态数据集。

Result: 实验结果表明，EMWM在CSI预测和环境重建方面均优于传统神经网络和大语言模型(LLM)基线。CSI预测实现0.9699的平方广义余弦相似度(SGCS)，在不同信噪比(SNR)条件下表现出鲁棒性，并在28GHz频率下实现零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: EMWM作为首个统一框架，为6G任务提供了建模基础，通过学习通用电磁表示，能够同时支持信道预测和环境重建，展现出在多模态感知和通信融合方面的潜力。

Abstract: The integration of sensing, communication, and intelligence is becoming a key enabler for sixth generation (6G) wireless systems, where intelligent terminals are expected to simultaneously support efficient link establishment and reliable environmental sensing. However, existing studies mainly exploit sensing information or communication information to address a single task, such as channel prediction or environment reconstruction. Motivated by the shared dependence of optical and radio-frequency signals on the surrounding environment, we propose the electromagnetic world model (EMWM), the first unified framework for joint environment reconstruction and channel prediction. EMWM learns a common electromagnetic representation with the potential to provide a modeling foundation for 6G tasks. Specifically, partial channel state information (CSI) and multi-view red-green-blue (RGB) images are encoded into CSI and visual tokens and jointly processed by a hierarchical world-model backbone with local and global aggregation. Based on the learned representation, a mixture-of-experts (MoE)-based CSI prediction head reconstructs the complete CSI, while a depth prediction head estimates multi-view depth maps that are further converted into three-dimensional (3D) point clouds. Moreover, a large-scale multi-modal dataset is constructed based on a campus digital twin. Experimental results show that EMWM outperforms conventional neural network and large language model (LLM) baselines in both CSI prediction and environment reconstruction, achieving a squared generalized cosine similarity (SGCS) of 0.9699 for CSI prediction while demonstrating robustness across different signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) conditions and zero-shot generalization at 28 GHz.

</details>
